## 1. Purpose and scope

The aim of the project is a design of the plant that a workshop can build using only the files in this repository. This page is the master list of the work still needed to reach that aim. It lists every task, the condition that closes it, the tasks it depends on and, for a closed task, the file that proves it is done. It is written for an engineer who wants to see what is finished, what is open and what to work on next.

A task is marked done only when a file in the repository proves it, and that file is cited next to the task. A study value, an envelope dimension or a figure on hold does not close a task. Where the evidence is partial, the task stays open and carries a status note. This page reports the state of the files; it introduces no new data.

This list supersedes `internal/MASTER_PLAN.md` (AQS-PLAN-004) as the list of work. MASTER_PLAN remains the record of how each line of the earlier plan was closed. Three other documents govern parts of the work:

- `PROJECT_PLAN.md` (AQS-PLAN-005) sets the engineering limits and the order of the phases.
- `MATERIALS_STD.md` (AQS-STD-002) is the materials rule applied in unit task 09.
- `MFG_MODEL_PLAN.md` (AQS-PLAN-006) governs the computer-aided design (CAD) models of unit task 10.

AQS-PLAN-001 to AQS-PLAN-003, AQS-MFG-001, AQS-REM-001, AQS-REM-BLD-001, AQS-MVS-PLAN-001 and AQS-MVS-CHK-001 are earlier plans kept as history.

The tasks are of two kinds. Cross-cutting tasks (section 7) apply to the plant as a whole and carry identifiers of the form X-GOV-04. Unit tasks (section 8) apply to one unit of the plant. The units are the 182 present nodes of `flow_graph/plant_flow_graph.json`, grouped by system. An accounting node, which records a plant boundary and has no equipment, carries task 01 only.

## 3. Triage of open tasks

Every open task carries a `Triage:` tag that says what is needed to close it:

- **desk**: the task can be closed from files in this repository.
- **hardware**: the task needs a built item, a test, a site measurement or an as-built record.
- **owner**: the task waits on a decision by the project owner, recorded in `OPEN_QUESTIONS.md` (AQS-OQ-001).

Desk tasks have a priority from P1 (first) to P5 (last):

- P1: the process basis, sizing, and cross-cutting tasks on the critical path.
- P2: piping and instrumentation diagrams (P&IDs), nozzles, lines, relief, and the other cross-cutting tasks.
- P3: internals, instruments, materials, and the production plans for the inventories.
- P4: CAD models, fabrication drawings, the bill of materials (BOM), and the fabrication route.
- P5: installation.

Unit task 15 (test and commission) is a hardware task. Its procedure can be written at a desk, but its acceptance record needs the built unit. Table 3.1 gives the number of open tasks under each tag.

Table 3.1. Open tasks by triage tag

| Triage | Open tasks |
| --- | --- |
| desk P1 | 4 |
| desk P2 | 24 |
| desk P3 | 34 |
| desk P4 | 472 |
| desk P5 | 179 |
| hardware | 188 |
| owner | 3 |

## 5. Critical path and phase sequence

The work runs in nine phases. Each phase lists the tasks it contains.

1. Phase A, design basis: X-GOV-04, X-BAS-02, X-BAS-03, X-BAS-04, X-MAT-01 and X-MAT-06. No unit downstream is sized until the bounding cases and the site are fixed.
2. Phase B, process: unit task 02 for every unit, starting with the metals line (AQS-PLAN-002 section 4), then the load list (X-ELS-02) and the heat ladder (X-THM-01).
3. Phase C, P&IDs and safety: unit task 03, X-CTL-01, the hazard and operability study (HAZOP, X-SAF-01), relief sizing (X-SAF-04) and hazardous area classification (X-ELS-09).
4. Phase D, lines and materials: unit tasks 05 and 09, X-PIP-01, X-PIP-02 and X-MAT-02.
5. Phase E, mechanical design: unit tasks 06, 10 and 12 in the tier order of AQS-PLAN-006, and the structural loads (X-BLD-02).
6. Phase F, structure and layout: X-BLD-04, X-BLD-01, X-BLD-03, X-PIP-03, X-THM-02 and X-SAF-05.
7. Phase G, electrical and control detail: X-ELS-03 to X-ELS-08 and X-CTL-02 to X-CTL-04.
8. Phase H, design package: unit tasks 11 and 13, and the X-DWG, X-PRO, X-QA-01 and X-COD tasks.
9. Phase I, build: the X-ASM tasks, unit task 14, the X-COM tasks, unit task 15, the X-OPS tasks, X-QA-03 and the X-END tasks.

The critical path is the chain of tasks that sets the earliest finish date. It runs: X-BAS-02, then unit task 02 for the metals units (LEACH_CSTR_1 and EW_1 to EW_9), X-ELS-02, X-ELS-04, unit task 03, X-SAF-01, X-PIP-02, unit task 10, X-BLD-02, X-BLD-01 and X-BLD-03, unit tasks 11 and 13, X-ASM-01 and X-COM-01.

Depends: X-RTE-POLY_TO_FAB. Triage: hardware.

Done when: an azide route from plant streams (hydrazine from ammonia and hypochlorite, then the nitrite azide synthesis) and the aromatic amine and ketone it is built on are sized and closed, with an organic developer for LITHO_DEV, or the negative class is dropped (A-M-432). Depends: X-RTE-POLY_TO_FAB. Triage: desk P2.
- [ ] **X-RTE-COMB-HEAT-FUEL** Fuel route for the fuel-fired alternate COMB_HEAT. Done when: COMB_HEAT gets a fuel route from a plant fuel (the C5-C10 cut or the chlorinator CO) and its off-gas route back, sized at the outage of the electric furnace it backs up, or the unit is retired (A-M-433). Depends: X-RTE-RETIRED-43. Triage: desk P2.
- [ ] **X-RTE-LEGACY-PFD** Remaining hand-drawn process sheets. Done when: each script in flow_graph/exporters/matplotlib/ that the regenerator does not run is checked for what it draws and who cites it, then retired or replaced by a graph view. Depends: —. Triage: desk P2.

## Appendix A. Hand-kept task list and progress record

This appendix keeps, as written, the list of next tasks and the record of progress that were maintained by hand in section 5 of this page until 2026-10-16. Section 6 replaces the list. The entries record decisions and values at the time they were made; where an entry conflicts with sections 7 and 8, sections 7 and 8 are current.

Tank, agitator and pump functions added 2026-10-15 (`flow_graph/param/sizing/tank.py`).
